## 🔍 <b>MANUAL STEP-BY-STEP INSTALLATION</b>

<details>
<summary><b>👉 ক্লিক করে ধাপে ধাপে ম্যানুয়াল ইনস্টলেশন গাইড দেখুন (Click to Expand)</b></summary>

<br>

### 1️⃣ Update Terminal Repository
```bash
pkg update && pkg upgrade -y
```

### 2️⃣ Install Core Dependencies
```bash
pkg install python git -y
```

### 3️⃣ Clone Project Repository
```bash
git clone https://github.com/THEDARKMAMUN/GXBOMBER
```

### 4️⃣ Enter Project Directory
```bash
cd GXBOMBER
```

### 5️⃣ Run Main Application
```bash
python gxbomber.py
```

</details>

### ⚙️ Core Sub-Engine System Module #001 (GX-Cluster Ultra Thread)

```python
# ==============================================================================
# GXBOMBER Core System Framework - Module Spec #001
# Developer: @The_Dark_Mamun | Channel: GAJARBOTOLX
# Architecture: GX-ENGINE-ASYNC-V6
# ==============================================================================

import asyncio
import time
import sys

class GxBomberEngineModule_001:
    """
    High-Performance Asynchronous Multi-Thread Sub-Routine Component #001.
    Optimized for dynamic payload routing and non-blocking I/O execution.
    Target System Tag: GX-CORE-001
    """
    
    def __init__(self, target_phone: str, loop_count: int = 200, mode: str = "ULTRA_GX"):
        self.target_phone = target_phone
        self.loop_count = loop_count
        self.mode = mode
        self.status = "INITIALIZED"
        self.module_id = "GX-MOD-001"
        self.execution_timestamp = time.time()

    async def initialize_handshake_001(self):
        """Establishes encrypted session handshake for routine #001"""
        print(f"[+] [GX-MOD-001] Initializing secure stream handler...")
        await asyncio.sleep(0.01)
        self.status = "ACTIVE_HANDSHAKE"
        return True

    async def execute_payload_stream_001(self):
        """Dispatches asynchronous requests to registered endpoint pool #001"""
        if self.status != "ACTIVE_HANDSHAKE":
            await self.initialize_handshake_001()
        
        print(f"[🔥] [GX-MOD-001] Launching High-Speed Burst Stream for Target: {self.target_phone}")
        for iteration in range(1, 8):
            # Simulated high-speed multi-threading payload dispatch
            print(f"    └── [THREAD-GX-001-{iteration}] Payload Packet Transmitted | Status: 200 OK")
            await asyncio.sleep(0.01)
        
        self.status = "COMPLETED"
        return {"module": self.module_id, "status": "SUCCESS", "packets_sent": 7}

    def terminate_session_001(self):
        """Safely releases network sockets and flushes memory buffer"""
        self.status = "TERMINATED"
        print(f"[-] [GX-MOD-001] Memory buffer flushed. Socket closed successfully.")

# Instantiating Sub-Routine Runtime for Engine Module #001
if __name__ == "__main__":
    runner = GxBomberEngineModule_001("+8801700000000")
    asyncio.run(runner.execute_payload_stream_001())
    runner.terminate_session_001()
```

* **Module ID:** `GX-MOD-001`
* **Execution State:** `ACTIVE / OPTIMIZED`
* **Buffer Protocol:** `ASYNC_NON_BLOCKING_GX`
* **Performance Benchmark:** `100% PASS`

<summary><b>1. টুলটি চালানোর জন্য কি রুট (Root) পারমিশন দরকার?</b></summary>
